[EVENT: YEM79862]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Yemen | Location: Ibb - Jiblah Junction
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Actor2: Unidentified Armed Group (Yemen) [Political militia]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Khabar News Agency (Yemen); Yemen Data Project [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, clashes erupted between Houthi Traffic Control Police forces and a local gunman in Al Muwasalat Junction near Ibb - Jiblah Junction (Al Mashannah). There were no injuries or fatalities. The clashes erupted a day after Houthi Traffic Control Police forces from Sanaa deployed in Ibb city amid increased tensions in the city after an activist passed away and was presumably killed in Houthi prisons around 19 March.

---

Actor2: Military Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]

DESCRIPTION:

The clashes resulted in the injury of 4 gunmen (3 from Yahya Zayid and 1 from Muhammad Zayid).

[EVENT: YEM110722]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Yemen | Location: Abu Musa al Ashaari Military Camp
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Air/drone strike | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Actor2: West Coast Joint Forces [Political militia]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, Houthi forces clashed with the WCJF, including the 1st Zaraniq Brigade (coded under the Tihama Resistance), in Al Haymah area (At Tuhayat, Al Hudaydah). During the clashes, 5 explosives-laden Houthi drones targeted WCJF military sites, including part of the Abu Musa al Ashaari Military Camp (Al Khawkhah, Al Hudaydah), while 3 other Houthi drones were shot down. The clashes took place over the period of 27 and 28 March, and 1 April. 1 WCJF soldier of the 1st Zaraniq Brigade was killed and 2 others were wounded. Fatalities split across 6 events. 0 fatality coded to this event.

[EVENT: YEM79886]
Date: 2023-04-03
Country: Yemen | Location: Jabal am Sanad
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - STC Security Belt Forces [State forces]
Actor2: Military Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - STC Support and Reinforcement Brigades [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Aden al Ghad; Al Masdar; Crater Sky; Twitter; Undisclosed Source; Yemen Data Project; YNP [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 3 April 2023, STC Security Belt forces backed by Al As Salihi tribesmen clashed with the 5th STC Support and Reinforcement Brigades in Am Furayd, Jabal am Sanad, and Kawkab (Mudiyah, Abyan), resulting in the killing of 1 STC Security Belt soldier and the injury of 11 tribesmen and Security Belt soldiers. According to some sources, there were 2 fatalities, who were 1 tribesman and 1 nephew of the district director. Total fatalities coded as at least 1 and split across 3 events. 0 fatality coded to this event. The clashes erupted after earlier clashes between SRB and SBF and tribesmen resulted in the killing of the SBF commander of Mudiyah district, who was also an Al As Salihi tribesman, earlier that day (coded as a separate event). Tribal mediation ended the clashes.

[EVENT: YEM80127]

DESCRIPTION:
On 21 April 2023, STC 6th Shabwani Defense Brigade forces opened fire on the vehicle of an Islah-affiliated imam, who was also a Musabin tribesman and the director-general of the local Health and Population Office, in Bayhan al Ulya (Bayhan, Shabwah) killing 1 (the imam) and injuring 6 others including the imam's son and his brother. The reason behind the killing is that the imam had defied a ban on him preaching and resisted arrest, which led to the 6th Brigade of the STC Shabwani Defense forces firing on his car. Shabwah police forces arrested 8 soldiers from the Brigade and Shabwah governor issued a decree establishing an investigation committee comprising the heads of several Shabwah security bodies. The Musabin tribe and other Bayhan tribes organized a sit-in two days later to demand retribution for the killing (coded as a separate event).

[EVENT: YEM80175]
Date: 2023-04-28
Country: Yemen | Location: Coast of Huswain
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Private Security Forces (India) [External/Other forces]
Actor2: Military Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - Coast Guard [State forces]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: 2 December News; Aden al Ghad; AP; Mareb Press; South24; UN Panel of Experts; Undisclosed Source; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 28 April 2023, private security guards of luxury yacht My Kalizma pleasure craft (IMO: 2869864) opened fire on IRG Coast Guard forces off the Coast of Huswain (Al Mahrah), leading to clashes between them. The clashes resulted in 1 fatality on the side of the Coast Guard and 1 injury in the side of the private security. The IRG Coast Guard claimed that the yacht was not flying a flag and did not answer calls after it entered Yemeni territorial waters which forced them to make an approach after which individuals on the yacht started shooting at their boat. A member of the private security claimed that they clashed with pirates who initiated the clashes rather than Coast Guard forces. The yacht was seized by Djibouti Navy forces on 7 May after an official request delivered to Djibouti from the Yemeni embassy. The yacht's crew included 10 Indian nationals and its armed security team consisting of 1 Greek and 2 Indian nationals.

[EVENT: YEM80320]
Date: 2023-05-10
Country: Yemen | Location: Sayun
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Aden al Ghad; Hadramawt 21; Nukhba Hadramawt; Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 10 May 2023, protesters at the call of the STC-affiliated Youth of Anger movement held a large demonstration in Sayun city (Hadramawt) to show support for the STC and its President Aydarus al-Zubaydi and to celebrate the success of the Southern Consultative Meeting (SCM). STC leaders and youth and civil organizations supporting the STC took part in the demonstration. The SCM was held in Aden over 4-8 May at the call of STC president gathering southern political, social, and tribal forces. It concluded by reaching an agreement over the political foundations of a new federal Southern state.

Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence

Actor2: al-Uzayba Tribal Militia (Yemen) [Identity militia]

Sources differed as to the origin of the clash with some claiming that the clash erupted as STC forces from the Dar Sad security forces under the command of Musleh al-Dharhani raided the house of a tribal sheikh -- Ali Ubayd al-Uzaybi -- after his guards shot down a reconnaissance drone and one source claiming that the clash was in fact between STC forces and a Houthi sleeper cell.

On 21 May 2023, workers from the Oil Exploration and Production Authority held a protest in front of the authority's headquarters in Aden - Yemen Petroleum Company (Al Mualla, Aden) due to delays in the payment of their salaries. Demonstrators also complained of low wages and poor administrative accountability. They threatened to strike until their salaries were paid in full, along with a restructuring of wages and benefits including health care, but returned to work after a promise from the Minister of Oil and Minerals to settle the dispute.

Around 21 May 2023, Houthi security forces abducted over 20 civilians in Uzlat Khabaz region (Al Udayn, Ibb) in a campaign led by the Houthi supervisor (mushrif) of Al Udayn district. The source claimed that he was involved in tribal disputes in the area.

[EVENT: YEM80489]
Date: 2023-05-21
Country: Yemen | Location: Al Kudamah
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Remote explosive/landmine/IED | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: 2 December News; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 21 May 2023, Houthi forces blew up a civilian house in Al Kudamah (Maqbanah, Taizz) with dynamite. There were no injuries or fatalities. The reason behind the incident was not reported although the source noted that Houthi forces have been blowing up houses as a retaliatory practice against its opponents.
